Algorithm:
```
for iter:
 b=conv(a)
 p, J = lse_p(b,beta)
 G = grad_w(a,p)
 Gc=G - mean(G)
 line search alpha: start alpha stored; for up to maybe 5:
   v=a - alpha*Gc
   a_new=proj(v)
   J_new=lse_value(conv(a_new), beta)
   if J_new < J - curl: accept, alpha*=1.5; break
   alpha*=0.5
 if no accept: perturb/restart
 update best by exact score maybe every few iter if J low
```

LP direction method:
Implement `_lp_direction(a, M, n, L)`:
- Build A matrix (2n-1)x n. For linear constraints A g ≤ M. A_{k,j}=a_{k-j} if 0≤k-j<n else0.
- Objective max sum(g) (c=-ones). Bounds g in [0, B] with B maybe 10 (since optimal sum likely O(1)). If infeasible due numerical, fallback a.
- `linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(2n-1,M), bounds=(0,B), method='highs')`.
- If success, g=res.x; if sum small, fallback.
- Normalize g to sum1.
- Return g.
Then iterate:
```
for t in steps:
  M=scoremax(a)
  g=lp_direction(a,M)
  for alpha in [0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5,1.0]:
    h=(1-alpha)a+alpha*g
    h=normalize
    if score(h)<score(a)*0.999: accept
  update best
  maybe mix with best
```
Can this reduce? If g is not a, h changes distribution. Since a*g≤M and a*a≤M, h*a≤M, but h*h unknown. But line search exact.
LP expensive. We can call at most a few times. Use for n=256 only. Build A efficiently via `scipy.linalg.toeplitz`? Use numpy construction: A[k,j]=a[k-j] for j≤k; for k<n, A[k,:] shift; for k≥n, A[k,j]=a[k-j] where k-j<n. Could do with loops n=256 fine. Or use `np.zeros`; loops over j: col = a[max(0,k-n+1):...]. Simple O(n^2) per LP; building not bottleneck.
- We need import scipy.optimize as maybe. If linprog unavailable, skip.

Local optimizer 1: L-BFGS on log variables with LSE.
Pseudo:
```
def _optimize_lbfgs(a, deadline, rng, beta=40.0, maxiter=500):
    n=a.size
    z = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-12)) - np.log(n)  # any
    best_a=a; best_score=score(a)
    val, grad = objective(z,beta)
    res = minimize(_obj_with_jac, z, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, args=(beta,), options={'maxiter':..., 'ftol':1e-8, 'gtol':1e-6, 'maxls':20})
    # after res, if improved update
    # repeat with higher beta from current
```
But `minimize` internal line search maybe many evals; set time? Can't easily check time inside objective? Could add global deadline and return large? Better run fixed maxiter; each eval FFT O(nlogn), n=512 maxiter500 =>250k eval? Too much. N=256, 500 eval okay (FFT 512). But L-BFGS may need hundreds. For 2400s, many runs. We can set maxiter=200 per beta. It may be fine.
- However objective with β=40 might be too flat; β increasing. Use β sequence [10,30,100,300,1000]. LSE at high β approximates max but can be nonconvex with sharp minima. BFGS may step poorly. We can use line search trust.
- We can implement our own log-gradient with line search, more controllable.

Log-space gradient step:
Parameters z, a=softmax(z). G_a gradient of J wrt a (LSE). G_z = a*(G_a - dot(a,G_a)). Since J not homogeneous. Take step z_new = z - α G_z. a_new=softmax(z_new) (sum1 automatically). Line search on J. No projection needed; positivity enforced. This may be better than simplex projection. Softmax may underflow for extreme z; initialize z from log a - mean; keep scale moderate by subtracting max. We can renormalize z? softmax invariant to shift; line search α small.
- For exact max, gradient not defined; use LSE.
- To avoid softmax drift (some a_i extremely small causing a*G underflow), use adaptive floor and reset z = log(max(a,eps)) with renormalization? Softmax of large differences can underflow for n small okay. Could use `z = np.log(a + 1e-300)` each iter? That collapses? Better maintain z but occasionally recenter: z-=mean(z).
- Use gradient scaling: scale G_z by its norm or max. α adapt.
- Line search: compute J. If fail, reduce α and maybe perturb.
This is similar Fibonacci but on log manifold.

We can combine with exact score: If exact score improves, update best even if J not monotone.

Optimizer loop for log-gradient:
```
def _refine_log(a, deadline, rng, beta_start=10):
    a=normalize(a); best=a; best_score=score(a)
    z=log(a)-mean(log(a))
    beta=beta_start
    alpha=1.0/(norm(G_z)+1e-12) maybe
    stale=0
    while time<deadline:
       val,val_grad, a = compute from z (softmax)
       Gz=a*(G - dot(a,G))
       gn=np.linalg.norm(Gz)
       if small break
       alpha = min(2, alpha*(1+ ...)/?)
       improve=False
       for ls in range(10):
          zn=z-step*Gz/ (alpha? )
          aan=softmax(zn)
          new=J(aan)
          if new<val: accept; break
          step*=0.5
       if not accept: perturb z by noise; reset beta lower
       every 50 its: beta*=1.5 up to 1000
       check exact score, update best
       if stale large: restart from best with noise
```
Need compute J multiple times. We can use FFT b and LSE. For exact score, use max b maybe with FFT. We don't need direct.

Softmax function stable.

Potential issue: LSE objective for sum-normalized a is not scale invariant but z softmax already normalized. Good.

Optimizer 2: LP-direction.
Let's think if LP can solve global if we iterate and update M? Algorithm:
```
def _lp_refine(a, deadline, rng):
  a=normalize(a); best...
  for it in range(200):
    b=conv(a); M=maxb
    g=lp_direction(a,M)
    # line search along g from a for exact score
    best_t=0; best_val=score(a)
    for t in [0.01,0.025,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5,0.7,1.0]:
       h=normalize((1-t)*a+t*g)
       s=score(h)
       if s<best_val*(1-1e-8): ...
    if no improve, try g from M_new? or perturb
```

Optimizer 3: Active set exact line search.

```
for it:
  b=conv(a); m=max; top = np.argsort(b)[-T:]; w=uniform top (or softmax β)
  G=grad_w(a,w)
  Gz = a*(G - dot(a,G))
  alpha adapt; step z
```

Function:
```
def _refine_log(a, end_time, rng, label="", beta_init=8.0):
    a=_normalize(a)
    n=a.size
    if n<64: a=_resample(_made..., 256)? maybe
    best=a.copy(); best_score=_score(a)
    z=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))
    z-=np.mean(z)
    beta=beta_init
    step=0.05 maybe scaled
    fail=0; stale=0; it=0
    # precompute fft sizes
    Lb=pow2(2*n-1)
    Lg=pow2(3*n-2)
    while time<end_time:
       a=softmax(z)  # sum1
       b=autocorr(a,Lb)
       # choose weights
       use_top = (it%3==0)
       if use_top:
          k_top=min(max(1,int(n/16)), b.size)
          idx=np.argpartition(b, -k_top)[-k_top:]
          w=np.zeros(b.size); w[idx]=1/k_top
          smooth_val=np.max(b)
       else:
          w, smooth_val=_lse_p(b,beta)
       G=weighted_gradient(a,w,Lg,n)
       Gz=a*(G - np.dot(a,G))
       norm=np.linalg.norm(Gz)
       if not finite or norm<1e-16: perturb
       if step <= 1e-8 or step > 2: reset
       improved=False
       for _ in range(16):
          zn=z - step*Gz/norm? # normalize gradient by norm for scale invariance
          if np.max(zn)-np.min(zn)>1e6: break
          an=softmax(zn)
          # compute J with same weights? For line search need objective. Simpler use same smooth objective recomputed with p? But weights fixed, approximate. Use LSE value if not top? For line search use exact-ish: if use_top use max(b)?? Better use LSE value with current beta for all.
          b2=autocorr(an,Lb)
          # For acceptance, use smooth objective J_lse with beta (or max if beta large). To reduce FFT, need b2.
          J2=_lse_val(b2,beta)
          if J2 < smooth_val - 1e-15*max(1,abs(smooth_val)):
              z=zn; a=an; improved=True; break
          step*=0.5
       if improved:
          step=min(2.0, step*1.1)
          fail=0
          # update exact score occasionally
          exact=_score_from_b(b2) if accepted else _score(a)
          if exact<best_score* (1-1e-10): best=copy; best_score=exact
          stale=0
       else:
          fail+=1
          # if fail large, perturb z
          if fail>40:
             # recenter a to best and add noise
             base=best if rng.random()<0.5 else a
             a2=normalize(base * np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.1,size=n)))
             z=np.log(a2); z-=mean
             step=0.05; beta=max(8,beta*0.5); fail=0; stale+=1
       # beta schedule: if improved and smooth near max, increase
       if it%50==0:
          beta=min(1000,beta*1.3)
       if it%100==0 and best_score > target? ...
       it+=1
    # final exact from best; maybe polish with direct?
    return best,best_score
```
Need compute `smooth_val` used in line search. If using top weights, maybe compute J_lse anyway. We can compute J_lse for b always (we have b). Then gradient uses top or LSE weights, but line search uses J_lse. Good.
- Acceptance based on J_lse, not top weighted. Good.
- Exact score update every accepted step or every 5. Could be costly? b2 already computed; exact max from b2 cheap O(Lb) maybe. Use `score=2*n*max(b2)` (sum1). No need additional convolution. Good.

Important: We need ensure that if sequence has zeros, log a underflows. Use `a = softmax(z)` positive; z from log(max(a, eps)). But softmax of z can produce very small values >0. Good.

Initial z: `z = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300) - mean)`. If some zero, set to -large. But within log-refine, a may become zero? Softmax positive unless underflow. Use eps 1e-12? Could restrict support. To allow zeros, use `a = a + 1e-12; normalize` each time? Softmax with z clipped. We can do `a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12); a/=a.sum()` before log. This adds tiny mass to zeros. Fine.

Line search step scaling: `Gz` magnitude. Use `step=0.1/ (norm+1e-12)` or maintain based on norm. Let's use normalized gradient `Gz_norm = Gz / max(1e-8, norm)` and step α (dimensionless). Initialize α=0.2. On accept α*=1.1 up to 10; fail α*=0.5. This makes step relative.

Perturbations:
```
def _perturb(a, rng, strength=0.15):
   if rng.random()<0.3:
      # block multiplicative
      width=max(2,n//16)
      idx0=rng.integers(0,n-width+1)
      a2=a.copy(); a2[idx0:idx0+width]*=exp(rng.normal(0,strength,width))
   else:
      noise=exp(rng.normal(0,strength,size=n))
      a2=a*noise + 1e-6*uniform
   return normalize(a2)
```
Also mirror: `a2=0.5*(a+a[::-1])` sometimes, but mirror may increase symmetric peaks. Could use occasionally.

```
def _make_mixture(N, p):
   # p length5: floor, left, right, al1, al2
   floor=np.exp(p[0]); left=np.exp(p[1]); right=np.exp(p[2]); al1=0.05+np.exp(p[3]); al2=0.05+np.exp(p[4])
   x=np.linspace(0,1,N)
   f=floor + left*np.exp(-al1*x) + right*np.exp(-al2*(1-x))
   # maybe add smooth random? no
   return normalize(f)
```

LP implementation details:
- `A_a` construction efficient: For k=0..2n-2, row length n. A_a[k, j] = a[k-j] if 0≤k-j<n. We can construct as:
```
A=np.zeros((2*n-1,n))
for j in range(n):
   col=a.copy()
   # g_j contributes to b_{i+j} for i=0..n-1 => row k=i+j
   A[j:j+n, j]=a
```
Check: (a*g)_k = ∑_i a_i g_{k-i}; j=k-i -> i=k-j. For each g_j, contribution a_i at row i+j. So column j has a at rows j..j+n-1. Yes. Great O(n^2) vectorized: loop j assign slice. n=512 => 512 slices length512, fine.
- Constraint: A g ≤ M * (1? sumg?) If g not normalized. Since M is max a*a with sum a=1. For g scale s, a*g scales s, constraint scale. If we maximize sum, optimal g has scale ~? We can set objective max sum, bounds g≤B. If B too small, direction clipped; B=10. Since a sum1 feasible, OPT≥1. The direction after normalization independent of scale. If optimum hits B for some coord, maybe not ideal, but B large enough.
- Use `linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(rows,M*1.0001), bounds=[(0,B)]*n, method='highs')`. `c=-np.ones(n)`. If n large (1024) A 2047x1024 ~2M, linprog maybe too slow. Use LP only for n≤512 or maybe 1024 few times. We can limit n<=512.
- If success but `sum(g)<1e-8`, return a.
- Normalize g. Also mix with uniform eps to avoid zeros: g=0.99*g+0.01/n.

`_lp_refine(a, end, rng, max_iter=30)`:
```
best=a,best_score=score(a)
for it in range(max_iter):
  b=autocorr; M=maxb
  g=lp_direction(a,M)
  if g is None: break
  # line search t: try [1.0,0.7,0.5,0.3,0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01]
  best_t=0; best_s=best_score or score(a)
  for t ...:
     h=normalize((1-t)*a+t*g)
     s=score(h)
     if s<best_aux: ...
  if best_t>0 and best_s<score(a)*(1-1e-10): a=h; update best
  else:
     # try smoother g? or perturb a
     if it%3==0: a=perturb(a)
     else break after stale
  if time>end break
return best,best_score
```

`_autocorr(a,L)`:
```
A=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
b=np.fft.irfft(A*A,L)
return b[:2*a.size-1]
```
A*A since same. Note many FFT libs output small imaginary? irfft real. Good.

`_weighted_gradient(a,w,Lg,n)`:
```

h=conv[n-1:2*n-1]
return 2.0*negative clip? h maybe tiny negative; clip to 0? Derivative should be nonnegative if a,w nonnegative, so clip negative to0.
return 2.0*np.maximum(h,0.0)
```
Check length: Lg >= n + len(w)-1 = n + (2n-1)-1=3n-2. Good.

`_lse_val(b,beta)`:
```
m=np.max(b)
if beta<=1: return m
arg=beta*(b-m); arg=clip(arg,-745,0)
s=np.exp(arg).sum()
if s<=0: return m
return m + np.log(s)/beta
```
`_lse_p` similar returns p, val.

Softmax:
```
def _softmax(z):
  z=z-np.max(z)
  e=np.exp(np.clip(z,-745,0))
  s=e.sum()
  if s<=0: return np.full(z.size,1/z.size)
  return e/s
```

```
def _coordinate_polish(a, end, rng, passes=0):
 a=normalize; n=a.size; best=a; best_score=score(a)
 factor=1.0
 for pass in range(passes or until no improve/time):
   moved=False
   order=np.arange(n) maybe rng.permutation
   for i in order:
     if time>end break
     vals = best[i]
     candidates = [0.0, vals*0.0, vals*0.25, vals*0.5, vals*0.75, vals*1.25, vals*1.5, vals*2.0]
     if vals==0: candidates=[0,1e-6/n,1e-5/n,1e-4/n]
     best_val=best_score; best_v=vals
     for v in candidates:
        a2=best.copy(); a2[i]=max(0,v); a2=normalize(a2)
        s=score(a2) # FFT
        if s<best_val: ...
     if best_v != vals: best[i]=best_v; best=normalize(best); best_score=best_val; moved=True
   if not moved: break
 return best,best_score
```


`_next_pow2(size)`:
```
size=int(size)
if size<=1: return 1

```

`_sanitize(a)`:
```
a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()

a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
return a
```

```
a=_sanitize(a)

if s<=1e-300: return np.ones(a.size)/a.size
return a/s
```


`_refine_log` as described. Let's code carefully.

```
def _refine_log(a, end_time, rng, label=""):
    a=_normalize(a)
    n=a.size
    if n < 32:
        a=_resample_sequence(a,256)
        n=256
    best_a=a.copy()
    best_score=_score_fft(a)
    # z
    z=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))
    z-=np.mean(z)
    beta_scale=3.0
    alpha=0.2
    fail=0; stale=0; it=0; last_improve=0
    Lb=_next_pow2(2*n-1); Lg=_next_pow2(3*n-2)
    while time.monotonic() < end_time:
       a=_softmax(z)
       b=_autocorr(a,Lb)
       maxb=float(np.max(b))
       if not finite: break
       beta=min(5e6, max(2.0, beta_scale/maxb if maxb>0 else 1e6))
       smooth=_lse_val(b,beta)
       exact=2.0*n*maxb
       if finite and exact<best_score*(1-1e-12):
          best_score=exact; best_a=a.copy()
       # if very close to target? continue but no harm
       if beta > 1500:
          K=max(5, (2*n-1)//20)
          idx=np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]
          w=np.full(b.size, 1e-12)
          w[idx]=1.0
          w/=w.sum()
       else:
          w,_=_lse_p(b,beta)
       G=_weighted_gradient(a,w,Lg,n)
       Gz=a*(G - float(np.dot(a,G)))
       Gz-=float(np.mean(Gz))
       norm=float(np.linalg.norm(Gz))
       if not finite or norm<1e-15:
          a2=_smooth_perturb(best_a,rng,0.07)
          z=np.log(np.maximum(a2,1e-300)); z-=np.mean(z); alpha=0.2; fail=0; it+=1; continue
       gn=Gz/norm
       accepted=False; final_smooth=smooth; final_exact=exact; a_new=a; b_new=b
       for _ in range(14):
          zn=z - alpha*gn
          # dynamic range
          mn=np.max(zn); zn-=mn? no softmax stable subtract inside; but to prevent large, center mn=mean
          zc=zn - float(np.mean(zn))
          an=_softmax(zc)
          b2=_autocorr(an,Lb)
          s2=float(np.max(b2))
          sm2=_lse_val(b2,beta)
          if sm2 < final_smooth - 1e-15:
             z=zc; a_new=an; b_new=b2; final_smooth=sm2; final_exact=2.0*n*s2; accepted=True; break
          alpha *= 0.45
          if alpha < 1e-10: break
       if accepted:
          alpha=min(5.0, alpha*1.12)
          fail=0; last_improve=it
          # after accepting, z already centered; a=a_new
          if final_exact < best_score*(1-1e-12):
             best_score=final_exact; best_a=a_new.copy()
       else:
          fail+=1
          if fail > 35:
             # restart
             if rng.random() < 0.6:
                base=best_a
             else:
                base=a
             a2=_smooth_perturb(base,rng,0.08)
             z=np.log(np.maximum(a2,1e-300)); z-=np.mean(z)
             alpha=0.2
             beta_scale=max(1.0,beta_scale*0.6)
             fail=0; stale+=1; it+=1; continue
       # beta_scale schedule
       if it % 25 == 0:
          beta_scale = min(80.0, beta_scale * 1.18)
       if it - last_improve > 200 and it > 60:
          if rng.random() < 0.35:
             a2=_smooth_perturb(best_a,rng,0.05)
             z=np.log(np.maximum(a2,1e-300)); z-=np.mean(z)
             beta_scale=max(1.0, beta_scale*0.7)
             fail=0
       it += 1
    # final sanitize best
    best_a=_normalize(best_a)
    best_score=_score_fft(best_a)
    return best_a, best_score
```
Need define `_smooth_perturb` before refine? Python top-level order not required if defined before call at runtime (function definitions executed). OK.

One issue: after accepted, `a` variable not updated? We set `a_new` but loop top `a=_softmax(z)` next iteration, okay. If not accepted, `a` remains old. For exact best update, if no accept but current exact updated, okay.

But if fail multiple, after fail restart we didn't update `a` variable; next top recomputes from z? We set z new, so okay.

```
def _coordinate_polish(a,end,rng):
   a=_normalize(a); n=a.size
   best=a; best_score=_score_fft(a)
   passes=0
   no_improve=0
   while time<end-1 and passes<4:
      moved=False
      order=np.arange(n)
      rng.shuffle(order)? rng.permutation
      Lb=...
      for i in order:
        if time>end: break
        v=best[i]
        cands=[v*0.0,v*0.25,v*0.5,v*0.75,v*1.0]
        if v>0: cands += [v*1.3,v*1.8,v*2.5]
        else: cands=[0.0,1e-8/n,1e-7/n,1e-6/n]
        best_v=v; best_s=best_score
        for val in cands:
           a2=best.copy(); a2[i]=max(0.0,float(val)); a2=_normalize(a2)
           s=_score_fft(a2)
           if s < best_s:
              best_s=s; best_v=val
        if best_v != v:
           a2=best.copy(); a2[i]=max(0.0,best_v); a2=_normalize(a2); best=a2; best_score=best_s; moved=True
      passes+=1
      if not moved: break
   return best, best_score
```
This recomputes score for many candidates. To speed, if n>1024 skip or one pass. In main, call only if n<=1024 or time large.

LP refine:
```
def _lp_direction(a, M, Nmax=512):
   if _scipy_linprog is None: return None
   a=_normalize(a); n=a.size
   if n>Nmax: return None
   A=np.zeros((2*n-1,n), dtype=np.float64)
   for j in range(n):
      A[j:j+n, j]=a
   c=-np.ones(n)
   b_ub=np.full(2*n-1, M*1.0000001+1e-15)
   B=10.0
   bounds=[(0.0,B)]*n
   try:
      res=_scipy_linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds, method='highs')
   except Exception:
      try: res=_scipy_linprog(c, A_ub=A,b_ub=b_ub,bounds=bounds)
      except: return None
   if res is None or not res.success: return None
   g=res.x
   s=float(np.sum(g))
   if s<=1e-12: return None
   g=_normalize(g)
   # replace near-zero with small
   return g
```
`M` is maxb of a. If a has some zeros and M small, constraints for j where all a[i] in column j are zero may be 0*g≤M unbounded; bound B handles.
LP O(n^2) for n=512 A 1023x512 ~524k, fine. But `A[j:j+n,j]=a` loops j=512 slices, okay. For n=1024 ~1M maybe still okay but linprog slow; use Nmax=512.

`_lp_refine`:

```
def _lp_refine(a,end,rng,label=""):
   if _scipy_linprog is None: return normalize(a), score
   a=normalize; best; best_score
   max_iter=int(max(5, (end-time)*0.1))? Better fixed.
   it=0
   while time<end-5 and it<25:
      b=autocorr; M=max
      g=_lp_direction(a,M)
      if g is None: break
      a_cur=normalize((1-eps?)? no)
      best_t=0; best_s=score(a)
      base_s=best_s
      for t in (1.0,0.7,0.5,0.3,0.15,0.05,0.025,0.01):
         h=normalize((1-t)*a + t*g)
         s=score(h)
         if s<best_s:
             best_s=s; best_h=h; best_t=t
      if best_t>0:
         a=best_h
         if best_s<best_score*(1-1e-12): best=copy; best_score=best_s
         it+=1
      else:
         # try direction from smoothed LP? perturb a and break if stale
         a=_smooth_perturb(a,rng,0.04)
         it+=1
         if it>6: break
   return best,best_score
```

`_smooth_perturb`: Need if `base` all zero? normalize handles. Use `rng.normal`. Ensure finite.
```
def _smooth_perturb(a,rng,strength=0.1):
  a=_normalize(a); n=a.size; a2=a.copy()
  if rng.random()<0.35:
    width=max(2,n//16)
    start=int(rng.integers(0,n-width+1))
    multi=np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,strength,width))
    a2[start:start+width]*=multi
  else:
    multi=np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,strength,n))
    a2*=multi
  if rng.random()<0.12:
    a2=0.5*(a2+a2[::-1])
  a2=_sanitize(a2)
  s=a2.sum()
  if s<=0: return _make_uniform(n)
  return a2/s
```
Need `_make_uniform`.

`_power_transform`: If p close1. `a2=np.power(a+1e-15,p); normalize`. If p=0 returns uniform.
`_smooth_fft(a,cut=0.01?)`: Let's implement:
```
def _smooth_fft(a, keep=0.2):
 a=_normalize(a); n=a.size; F=np.fft.rfft(a)
 m=F.size; cut=max(2,int(m*keep))
 F= F.copy(); F[cut:]=0
 # taper: F[cut-1:cut] *=0.5? 
 inv=np.fft.irfft(F,n)
 inv=np.maximum(inv,0)
 s=inv.sum()
 if s<=0: return a
 return inv/s
```
Keep fraction of frequency bins to retain. If keep=0.2, smooth.

```
def _param_search(rng, end_time):
    low=np.array([...]); high=...
    best_a=None; best_s=1e100; best_p=None
    N=256
    # candidates list
    cand=[_default_mixture_params()]
    for i in range(3000):
       if time >= end_time: break
       p=rng.uniform(low,high)
       a=_mixture_sequence(p,N); s=_score_fft(a)
       if s<best_s: best...
       if len(cand)<8: cand.append(p) else maybe replace worst? not needed
    # local random around best at N=512
    N=512
    p=best_p if not None else cand[0]
    a=_mixture_sequence(p,N); best_s=_score_fft(a); best_a=a
    for it in range(4000):
       if time >= end_time: break
       step=0.25*(1.0+0.5*np.cos(0.5*it/4000))? simple 0.2
       p2=p + rng.normal(0.0,0.25,7)
       a2=_mixture_sequence(p2,N); s2=_score_fft(a2)
       if s2 < best_s:
          p=p2; best_s=s2; best_a=a2
       # if no improve every 200, maybe anneal? can continue
    # Optional Nelder if available
    if _scipy_minimize is not None and time.monotonic() < end_time - 30:
       try:
          res=_scipy_minimize(_mixture_score, p, args=(512,), method='Nelder-Mead', options={'maxiter':400, 'xatol':1e-6, 'fatol':1e-9})
          if res.fun < best_s:
             best_s=float(res.fun); p=res.x; best_a=_mixture_sequence(p,512)
       except Exception: pass
    if best_a is None: best_a=_make_uniform(256); best_s=2.0
    return best_a,best_s,p
```

```
   inits.append(_make_smooth_random?) define maybe _smooth_random(n,rng, 0.5)
   inits=_unique_inits(inits)
   used=0
   for init in inits[:num]:
      t_end=min(deadline-10, time.monotonic()+ps)
      if time.monotonic() >= t_end: break
      res_a,res_s=_refine_log(init,t_end,rng,label=f"n={n}")
      if finite and res_s < best_score:
         best_a=res_a; best_score=res_s; print
         # maybe try LP if n<=512 and time
      used+=1
   # after stage for n<=512, LP if time and score > target

       la, ls=_lp_refine(best_a.copy(), lp_end, rng, label="LP")

```

```
def _make_smooth_random(n,rng,amp=0.4):
 z=rng.normal(size=n)
 F=np.fft.rfft(z)
 cut=max(2,n//16); F=cut... ; f=irfft
 f=(f-np.mean)/std
 a=uniform * (1+amp*f); clip; normalize
```

Unique inits function:
```
def _unique_inits(inits):
 out=[]; seen=[]
 for a in inits:
  try:
    a=_normalize(a)
    if a.size<8: continue
    fp=tuple(np.round(a[::max(1,a.size//20)], 6).tolist())
    if fp not in seen:
       seen.append(fp); out.append(a)
  except: pass
 return out
```

```python
"""
Search for low-peak non-negative self-convolution coefficient sequences.

Objective (with sum normalized to 1):
    score(a) = 2 n max_k (a*a)_k

Strategy:
  * Keep several informative starts:
      - the provided height_sequence_1 if available,
      - resampled / transformed versions of the current best,
      - low-dimensional asymmetric "floor + left/right exponential/linear"
        densities,
      - smooth random starts.
  * Refine with a log-manifold smoothed projected-gradient method:
      - LSE surrogate of max(a*a), plus sharp top-active weights,
      - analytic FFT-based gradients,
      - line search in log-softmax variables.
  * Occasionally use an LP-direction mixing step for small sizes.
  * Upscale the best sequence to longer lengths and refine again.
  * Final coordinate polish and safe return.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as _scipy_minimize
except Exception:
    _scipy_minimize = None

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog as _scipy_linprog
except Exception:
    _scipy_linprog = None

np.seterr(over="ignore", under="ignore", invalid="ignore", divide="ignore")


def _next_pow2(size):
    """Smallest power of two >= size."""
    size = int(size)
    if size <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()


def _sanitize(a):
    """Return a finite nonnegative 1D float array."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return np.maximum(a, 0.0)


def _normalize(a):
    """Normalize to sum 1, nonnegative. Falls back to uniform if empty."""
    a = _sanitize(a)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    return a / s


def _make_uniform(n):
    n = max(1, int(n))
    return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)


def _make_exponential(n, alpha=1.0):
    n = max(2, int(n))
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    a = np.exp(-float(alpha) * x)
    return _normalize(a)


def _autocorr(a, fft_size):
    """Linear convolution a*a using zero-padded FFT."""
    a = _normalize(a)
    L = _next_pow2(max(2 * a.size - 1, int(fft_size)))
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    b = np.fft.irfft(A * A, L)
    return b[:2 * a.size - 1]


def _score_fft(a):
    """Fast FFT score for normalized a."""
    a = _normalize(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    b = _autocorr(a, _next_pow2(2 * n - 1))
    mb = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(mb):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * mb)


def _score_direct(a):
    """Direct np.convolve score, used only at the end for moderate sizes."""
    a = _normalize(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    if n > 8000:
        return _score_fft(a)
    b = np.convolve(a, a, mode="full")
    mb = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(mb):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * mb)


def _lse_value(b, beta):
    """Stable LSE(beta*b)/beta."""
    if b.size == 0:
        return 0.0
    m = float(np.max(b))
    if beta <= 1.0:
        return m
    arg = beta * (b - m)
    np.clip(arg, -745.0, 0.0, out=arg)
    e = np.exp(arg)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return m
    return m + float(np.log(s)) / float(beta)


def _lse_weights(b, beta):
    """Return weights proportional to exp(beta*b)."""
    if b.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    m = float(np.max(b))
    if beta <= 1.0:
        return np.full(b.size, 1.0 / b.size, dtype=np.float64)
    arg = beta * (b - m)
    np.clip(arg, -745.0, 0.0, out=arg)
    e = np.exp(arg)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(b.size, 1.0 / b.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def _weighted_gradient(a, weights, fft_size, n):
    """
    Gradient of sum_k weights_k * b_k wrt a_i.
    b_k = sum_{i+j=k} a_i a_j.
    """
    a = _normalize(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    ar = a[::-1]
    L = _next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
    A = np.fft.rfft(ar, L)
    W = np.fft.rfft(weights, L)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(A * W, L)
    h = conv[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    h = np.where(h > 0.0, h, 0.0)
    return 2.0 * h


def _softmax(z):
    """Stable softmax sum-to-one."""
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    z = z - float(np.max(z))
    z = np.clip(z, -745.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        n = max(1, z.size)
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return e / s


def _resample_sequence(old, new_n):
    """Interpolate a density from old length to new length using midpoints."""
    old = _normalize(old)
    new_n = max(2, int(new_n))
    if old.size == 0:
        return _make_uniform(new_n)
    if old.size == 1:
        return _make_uniform(new_n)
    if old.size == new_n:
        return old.copy()
    x_old = (np.arange(old.size, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(old.size)
    x_new = (np.arange(new_n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(new_n)
    new = np.interp(x_new, x_old, old)
    return _normalize(new)


def _power_transform(a, p):
    """Power transform of normalized nonnegative a."""
    a = _normalize(a)
    p = float(p)
    if p <= 0.0:
        return _make_uniform(a.size)
    a2 = np.power(a + 1e-15, p)
    return _normalize(a2)


def _mirror_mix(a, q):
    """Mirror blend: normalized(q*a + (1-q)*reversed(a))."""
    a = _normalize(a)
    q = float(np.clip(q, 0.0, 1.0))
    a2 = q * a + (1.0 - q) * a[::-1]
    return _normalize(a2)


def _smooth_fft(a, keep=0.2):
    """Light FFT low-pass smoothing."""
    a = _normalize(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n < 8:
        return _make_uniform(n)
    F = np.fft.rfft(a)
    m = F.size
    cut = max(2, int(m * float(np.clip(keep, 0.02, 0.9))))
    if cut < m:
        F[cut:] = 0.0
    F[cut - 1:cut] *= 0.5
    y = np.fft.irfft(F, n)
    y = np.maximum(y, 0.0)
    s = float(y.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return a
    return y / s


def _make_smooth_random(n, rng, amp=0.4):
    """Smooth random positive perturbation around uniform."""
    n = max(2, int(n))
    z = rng.normal(size=n)
    F = np.fft.rfft(z)
    cut = max(2, min(F.size, n // 16 + 2))
    if cut < F.size:
        F[cut:] = 0.0
    f = np.fft.irfft(F, n)
    f -= float(np.mean(f))
    sd = float(np.std(f))
    if sd > 0.0:
        f = f / sd
    base = _make_uniform(n)
    a = base * (1.0 + float(amp) * f)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
    return _normalize(a)


def _smooth_perturb(a, rng, strength=0.10):
    """Random multiplicative perturbation, optionally mirror-smoothed."""
    a = _normalize(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    a2 = a.copy()
    strength = float(max(1e-4, strength))
    if rng.random() < 0.35:
        width = max(2, n // 16)
        start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width + 1)))
        a2[start:start + width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, strength, width))
    else:
        a2 *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, strength, n))
    if rng.random() < 0.12:
        a2 = 0.5 * (a2 + a2[::-1])
    a2 = _sanitize(a2)
    return _normalize(a2)


def _unique_inits(inits):
    """Remove near-duplicate starts by a coarse fingerprint."""
    out = []
    seen = []
    for a in inits:
        try:
            a = _normalize(a)
            if a.size < 8:
                continue
            fp = tuple(np.round(a[::max(1, a.size // 20)], 6).tolist())
            if fp not in seen:
                seen.append(fp)
                out.append(a)
        except Exception:
            pass
    return out


def _default_mixture_params():
    """Parameters for the old good asymmetric floor + exponential start."""
    p = np.zeros(7, dtype=np.float64)
    p[0] = float(np.log(0.18))
    p[1] = 0.0
    p[2] = float(np.log(0.55))
    p[3] = float(np.log(max(1e-6, 1.0 - 0.05)))
    p[4] = p[3]
    p[5] = -30.0
    p[6] = -30.0
    return p


def _mixture_sequence(p, n):
    """
    Low-dimensional nonnegative density:
        f(x) = floor
             + L exp(-alpha1 x)
             + R exp(-alpha2 (1-x))
             + ll (1-x)
             + rl x
    """
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if p.size < 7:
        p = np.concatenate([p, -30.0 * np.ones(max(0, 7 - p.size))])
    p = p[:7]

    floor = float(np.exp(np.clip(p[0], -30.0, 30.0)))
    L = float(np.exp(np.clip(p[1], -30.0, 30.0)))
    R = float(np.exp(np.clip(p[2], -30.0, 30.0)))
    a1 = 0.05 + float(np.exp(np.clip(p[3], -30.0, 30.0)))
    a2 = 0.05 + float(np.exp(np.clip(p[4], -30.0, 30.0)))
    ll = float(np.exp(np.clip(p[5], -30.0, 30.0)))
    rl = float(np.exp(np.clip(p[6], -30.0, 30.0)))

    n = max(2, int(n))
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    f = floor + L * np.exp(-a1 * x) + R * np.exp(-a2 * (1.0 - x)) + ll * (1.0 - x) + rl * x
    return _normalize(f)


def _mixture_score(p, n):
    """Objective for scipy.minimize on the 7-parameter mixture."""
    try:
        a = _mixture_sequence(p, n)
        return _score_fft(a)
    except Exception:
        return 1e100


def _coordinate_polish(a, end_time, rng, max_passes=2):
    """Cheap coordinatewise one-variable searches."""
    a = _normalize(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    best = a.copy()
    best_score = _score_fft(best)
    if best_score >= 1e100:
        return best, best_score

    passes = 0
    full_L = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)

    while passes < max_passes and time.monotonic() < end_time - 1.0:
        moved = False
        order = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
        order = rng.permutation(order)

        for i in order:
            if time.monotonic() >= end_time - 1.0:
                break
            cur = float(best[i])
            cands = []
            if cur > 0.0:
                cands = [0.0, cur * 0.25, cur * 0.50, cur * 0.75,
                         cur * 1.30, cur * 1.90, cur * 2.80]
            else:
                cands = [0.0, 1e-8 / n, 1e-7 / n, 1e-6 / n, 1e-5 / n]

            loc_best = cur
            loc_score = best_score

            for val in cands:
                if time.monotonic() >= end_time - 1.0:
                    break
                val = float(max(0.0, val))
                a2 = best.copy()
                a2[i] = val
                a2 = _normalize(a2)
                b2 = _autocorr(a2, full_L)
                sc = float(2.0 * n * np.max(b2))
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < loc_score:
                    loc_score = sc
                    loc_best = val

            if loc_best != cur:
                best = best.copy()
                best[i] = loc_best
                best = _normalize(best)
                best_score = loc_score
                moved = True

        passes += 1
        if not moved:
            break

    best_score = _score_fft(best)
    return best, best_score


def _lp_direction(a, maxb, max_n=512, bound=10.0):
    """
    Solve: max sum(g) s.t. (a*g)_k <= maxb, 0 <= g_j <= bound.
    Returns normalized g or None.
    """
    if _scipy_linprog is None:
        return None
    a = _normalize(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n > int(max_n) or maxb <= 1e-300:
        return None

    A = np.zeros((2 * n - 1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for j in range(n):
        A[j:j + n, j] = a

    c = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    rhs = float(maxb) * (1.0 + 1e-9) + 1e-18
    b_ub = np.full(2 * n - 1, rhs, dtype=np.float64)
    bounds = [(0.0, float(bound))] * n

    res = None
    try:
        res = _scipy_linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds, method="highs")
    except Exception:
        try:
            res = _scipy_linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds)
        except Exception:
            return None

    if res is None or not getattr(res, "success", False):
        return None
    g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
    g = _sanitize(g)
    s = float(g.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return None
    return g / s


def _lp_refine(a, end_time, rng, max_iter=10):
    """Small-time LP-direction mixing refine."""
    a = _normalize(a)
    best = a.copy()
    best_score = _score_fft(best)
    n = int(a.size)
    L = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    it = 0
    stale = 0

    while it < int(max_iter) and time.monotonic() < end_time - 5.0:
        b = _autocorr(a, L)
        mb = float(np.max(b))
        g = _lp_direction(a, mb, max_n=max(n, 0), bound=10.0)
        if g is None:
            break

        if float(np.linalg.norm(g - a)) <= 1e-8:
            g = _smooth_perturb(g, rng, 0.05)

        base_score = _score_fft(a)
        best_t = 0.0
        best_t_score = base_score
        best_h = a.copy()

        for t in (1.0, 0.70, 0.50, 0.30, 0.15, 0.05, 0.02, 0.01):
            h = (1.0 - t) * a + t * g
            h = _normalize(h)
            sc = _score_fft(h)
            if sc < best_t_score:
                best_t_score = sc
                best_t = t
                best_h = h

        if best_t > 0.0 and best_t_score < base_score:
            a = best_h
            if best_t_score < best_score:
                best = a.copy()
                best_score = best_t_score
            stale = 0
        else:
            a = _smooth_perturb(a, rng, 0.03)
            stale += 1
            if stale >= 4:
                break

        it += 1

    a = _normalize(best)
    best_score = _score_fft(a)
    return a, best_score


def _refine_log(a, end_time, rng, label="refine"):
    """
    Log-softmax smoothed LSE / top-active gradient descent.
    Keeps the best exact sequence found.
    """
    a = _normalize(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n < 32:
        a = _resample_sequence(a, 256)
        n = int(a.size)

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = _score_fft(best_a)

    z = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    z -= float(np.mean(z))

    beta_scale = 3.0
    step = 0.20
    fail = 0
    it = 0
    last_improve = 0

    Lb = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    Lg = _next_pow2(3 * n - 2)

    while time.monotonic() < end_time:
        a = _softmax(z)
        b = _autocorr(a, Lb)
        mb = float(np.max(b))
        if not np.isfinite(mb) or mb <= 0.0:
            a = _smooth_perturb(best_a, rng, 0.06)
            z = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
            step = 0.20
            it += 1
            continue

        beta = min(5.0e6, max(2.0, beta_scale / max(1e-15, mb)))
        smooth = _lse_value(b, beta)
        exact = float(2.0 * n * mb)
        if np.isfinite(exact) and exact < best_score * (1.0 - 1e-12):
            best_score = exact
            best_a = a.copy()

        if beta > 1500.0:
            K = max(5, b.size // 20)
            idx = np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]
            w = np.full(b.size, 1e-12, dtype=np.float64)
            w[idx] = 1.0
            w /= float(w.sum())
        else:
            w = _lse_weights(b, beta)

        G = _weighted_gradient(a, w, Lg, n)
        Gz = a * (G - float(np.dot(a, G)))
        Gz -= float(np.mean(Gz))
        normG = float(np.linalg.norm(Gz))

        if not np.isfinite(normG) or normG <= 1e-15:
            a2 = _smooth_perturb(best_a, rng, 0.07)
            z = np.log(np.maximum(a2, 1e-300))
            z -= float(np.mean(z))
            step = 0.20
            it += 1
            continue

        gn = Gz / normG
        accepted = False
        accept_z = z
        accept_a = a
        accept_exact = exact
        accept_smooth = smooth

        tol = 1e-13 * max(1.0, abs(smooth))
        for _ in range(14):
            zn = z - step * gn
            zn -= float(np.mean(zn))
            rngz = float(np.max(zn) - np.min(zn))
            if rngz > 50.0:
                zn = (zn - float(np.mean(zn))) * (50.0 / rngz)

            an = _softmax(zn)
            b2 = _autocorr(an, Lb)
            mb2 = float(np.max(b2))
            if not np.isfinite(mb2):
                step *= 0.45
                continue
            sm2 = _lse_value(b2, beta)
            ex2 = float(2.0 * n * mb2)

            if sm2 < smooth - tol or ex2 < exact - 1e-13:
                accept_z = zn
                accept_a = an
                accept_exact = ex2
                accept_smooth = sm2
                accepted = True
                break
            step *= 0.45
            if step <= 1e-10:
                break

        if accepted:
            a = accept_a
            z = accept_z
            smooth = accept_smooth
            exact = accept_exact
            step = min(5.0, step * 1.12)
            fail = 0
            last_improve = it
            if exact < best_score * (1.0 - 1e-12):
                best_score = exact
                best_a = a.copy()
        else:
            fail += 1
            if fail > 35:
                base = best_a if rng.random() < 0.65 else a
                a2 = _smooth_perturb(base, rng, 0.08)
                z = np.log(np.maximum(a2, 1e-300))
                z -= float(np.mean(z))
                step = 0.20
                beta_scale = max(1.0, beta_scale * 0.6)
                fail = 0

        if it % 25 == 0:
            beta_scale = min(80.0, beta_scale * 1.16)

        if it - last_improve > 180 and it > 60 and rng.random() < 0.35:
            a2 = _smooth_perturb(best_a, rng, 0.05)
            z = np.log(np.maximum(a2, 1e-300))
            z -= float(np.mean(z))
            beta_scale = max(1.0, beta_scale * 0.7)
            fail = 0

        it += 1

    best_a = _normalize(best_a)
    best_score = _score_fft(best_a)
    return best_a, best_score


def _param_search(rng, end_time):
    """Search the low-dimensional mixture family."""
    if end_time <= time.monotonic():
        p = _default_mixture_params()
        a = _mixture_sequence(p, 256)
        return a, _score_fft(a), p

    low = np.array([-4.0, -4.0, -4.0, -1.0, -1.0, -4.0, -4.0], dtype=np.float64)
    high = np.array([1.0, 2.0, 2.0, 3.0, 3.0, 1.0, 1.0], dtype=np.float64)

    best_a = None
    best_s = 1e100
    best_p = _default_mixture_params()

    N = 256
    for _ in range(2500):
        if time.monotonic() >= end_time - 20.0:
            break
        p = rng.uniform(low, high)
        a = _mixture_sequence(p, N)
        s = _score_fft(a)
        if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_s:
            best_s = s
            best_a = a
            best_p = p

    N = 512
    if len(best_a) != N:
        pass
    a = _mixture_sequence(best_p, N)
    s = _score_fft(a)
    if np.isfinite(s):
        best_a = a
        best_s = s

    # Local annealed random search around best parameters.
    for it in range(3500):
        if time.monotonic() >= end_time - 15.0:
            break
        step = 0.28 * (0.35 + 0.65 * (1.0 - float(it) / 3500.0))
        p2 = best_p + rng.normal(0.0, step, 7)
        a2 = _mixture_sequence(p2, N)
        s2 = _score_fft(a2)
        if np.isfinite(s2) and s2 < best_s:
            best_s = s2
            best_a = a2
            best_p = p2

    # Optional local Nelder-Mead polish.
    if _scipy_minimize is not None and time.monotonic() < end_time - 20.0:
        try:
            res = _scipy_minimize(
                _mixture_score,
                best_p,
                args=(N,),
                method="Nelder-Mead",
                options={
                    "maxiter": 350,
                    "maxfev": 700,
                    "xatol": 1e-7,
                    "fatol": 1e-11,
                },
            )
            if res is not None and np.isfinite(res.fun) and float(res.fun) < best_s:
                best_s = float(res.fun)
                best_p = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
                best_a = _mixture_sequence(best_p, N)
        except Exception:
            pass

    if best_a is None:
        best_a = _mixture_sequence(best_p, 256)
        best_s = _score_fft(best_a)

    return best_a, best_s, best_p


def _get_given_sequence():
    """Fetch height_sequence_1 if present."""
    seq = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if seq is None:
        return None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None
    arr = _sanitize(arr)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if arr.size == 0 or s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    return arr / s


def propose_candidate(seed=0, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Main entry point.

    Runs within budget_s (seconds) and returns a list of nonnegative floats.
    """
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 2400.0
    if not np.isfinite(budget) or budget <= 0.0:
        budget = 2400.0
    deadline = t0 + max(5.0, budget - 30.0)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    print(f"[start] budget={budget:.1f}s deadline_in={deadline - t0:.1f}s", flush=True)

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # Initial best from a quick low-dimensional mixture search.
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    param_end = min(deadline - 900.0, t0 + max(15.0, 0.07 * budget))
    if param_end < t0 + 8.0:
        param_end = t0 + 8.0
    mix_a, mix_s, mix_p = _param_search(rng, param_end)
    best_a = mix_a
    best_score = mix_s
    print(f"[mix] len={best_a.size} score={best_score:.8f}", flush=True)

    given = _get_given_sequence()
    if given is not None:
        g = _normalize(given)
        gs = _score_fft(g)
        print(f"[given] len={g.size} fft_score={gs:.8f}", flush=True)
        if np.isfinite(gs) and gs < best_score:
            best_a = g
            best_score = gs

    if not np.isfinite(best_score) or best_a is None:
        best_a = _make_exponential(512, 1.0)
        best_score = _score_fft(best_a)
        print(f"[fallback exponential] score={best_score:.8f}", flush=True)

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # Staged search on increasing lengths.
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    scale = max(0.15, min(1.0, budget / 2400.0))

    # (length, number of starts, seconds per start)
    stages = [
        (256, 2, 35),
        (512, 2, 55),
        (1024, 2, 120),
        (2048, 3, 250),
        (4096, 2, 350),
    ]

    for n, num_starts, per_start in stages:
        per_start = int(max(15.0, float(per_start) * scale))
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - per_start * max(1, num_starts) * 0.35 - 25.0:
            continue

        # If resampling the current best to this length already helps, take it.
        if best_a is not None and best_a.size != n:
            rs = _resample_sequence(best_a, n)
            rs_s = _score_fft(rs)
            if np.isfinite(rs_s) and rs_s < best_score:
                best_a = rs
                best_score = rs_s
                print(f"[upsample] n={n} improved to {best_score:.8f}", flush=True)

        cands = []

        if given is not None:
            cands.append(_resample_sequence(given, n))
            cands.append(_power_transform(_resample_sequence(given, n), 0.85))
            cands.append(_smooth_fft(_resample_sequence(given, n), 0.25))

        if best_a is not None:
            rb = _resample_sequence(best_a, n)
            cands.append(rb)
            cands.append(_power_transform(rb, 0.85))
            cands.append(_power_transform(rb, 1.15))
            cands.append(_smooth_fft(rb, 0.25))
            cands.append(_mirror_mix(rb, 0.70))

        if mix_p is not None:
            cands.append(_mixture_sequence(mix_p, n))
            cands.append(_mixture_sequence(mix_p + rng.normal(0.0, 0.18, 7), n))

        cands.append(_make_exponential(n, float(rng.uniform(0.5, 3.0))))
        cands.append(_make_smooth_random(n, rng, float(rng.uniform(0.25, 0.70))))

        improved_candidates = []
        for c in cands:
            try:
                c = _normalize(c)
            except Exception:
                continue
            if c.size != n:
                c = _resample_sequence(c, n)
            s = _score_fft(c)
            if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_score:
                best_a = c
                best_score = s
                print(f"[init n={n}] score={s:.8f}", flush=True)
            improved_candidates.append(c)

        inits = _unique_inits(improved_candidates)

        for idx, init in enumerate(inits[:num_starts]):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 20.0:
                break
            t_end = min(deadline - 10.0, time.monotonic() + float(per_start))
            if t_end <= time.monotonic():
                break
            res_a, res_s = _refine_log(init.copy(), t_end, rng, label=f"n={n}")
            if np.isfinite(res_s) and res_s < best_score:
                best_a = res_a
                best_score = res_s
                print(f"[refine n={n} start {idx}] score={res_s:.8f}", flush=True)
            else:
                print(f"[refine n={n} start {idx}] no improve best={best_score:.8f}", flush=True)

        # Small LP polish for only small lengths.
        if (
            n <= 512
            and _scipy_linprog is not None
            and best_a is not None
            and int(best_a.size) <= 512
            and best_score > 1.5027
            and time.monotonic() < deadline - 80.0
        ):
            lp_end = min(deadline - 30.0, time.monotonic() + max(25.0, 60.0 * scale))
            la, ls = _lp_refine(best_a.copy(), lp_end, rng, max_iter=8)
            if np.isfinite(ls) and ls < best_score:
                best_a = la
                best_score = ls
                print(f"[LP polish] score={ls:.8f}", flush=True)

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # Final upscale / final refinement.
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    if best_a is not None and int(best_a.size) <= 2048 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 120.0:
        n_next = min(4096, max(2048, 2 * int(best_a.size)))
        init = _resample_sequence(best_a, n_next)
        init = _normalize(0.90 * init + 0.10 * _make_uniform(n_next))
        t_end = min(deadline - 8.0, time.monotonic() + 0.65 * max(30.0, deadline - time.monotonic()))
        res_a, res_s = _refine_log(init, t_end, rng, label="upscale_final")
        if np.isfinite(res_s) and res_s < best_score:
            best_a = res_a
            best_score = res_s
            print(f"[upscale_final] n={res_a.size} score={res_s:.8f}", flush=True)

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 8.0:
        res_a, res_s = _refine_log(best_a.copy(), deadline - 4.0, rng, label="final")
        if np.isfinite(res_s) and res_s < best_score:
            best_a = res_a
            best_score = res_s
            print(f"[final refine] score={res_s:.8f}", flush=True)
        else:
            print(f"[final refine] no improve best={best_score:.8f}", flush=True)

    # Coordinate polish for moderate sizes.
    if best_a is not None and int(best_a.size) <= 1024 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 12.0:
        res_a, res_s = _coordinate_polish(best_a.copy(), deadline - 3.0, rng, max_passes=2)
        if np.isfinite(res_s) and res_s < best_score:
            best_a = res_a
            best_score = res_s
            print(f"[coordinate polish] score={res_s:.8f}", flush=True)

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # Safe finalization and optional direct validation.
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    candidate = _normalize(best_a)
    candidate = _sanitize(candidate)
    try:
        ds = _score_direct(candidate)
        print(f"[direct final] len={candidate.size} direct_score={ds:.8f}", flush=True)
        if not np.isfinite(ds) and mix_a is not None:
            candidate = _normalize(mix_a)
            ds = _score_direct(candidate)
            print(f"[direct fallback] len={candidate.size} direct_score={ds:.8f}", flush=True)
    except Exception:
        candidate = _normalize(mix_a if mix_a is not None else _make_exponential(512, 1.0))

    try:
        ev = evaluate_sequence(candidate)
        print(f"[evaluate_sequence] {ev}", flush=True)
    except Exception:
        pass

    out = np.asarray(candidate, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    out = _sanitize(out)
    out = _normalize(out)
    return [float(x) for x in out]
```
